     Division and responsibilities                                                     分工及職責
     The Board is headed by the Chairman whose role differs from that of               董事會由主席領導，其職務有別於本公
     the Managing Director of the Company. The roles with the Chairman                 司董事總經理。主席及董事總經理各有
     and Managing Director are segregated and are not exercised by the                 獨立職務，且非由同一名人士擔任。
     same individual.

     The duties of the Chairman include (but not limited to) the following:            主席職責包括但不限於：

     •       scheduling meetings that enable the Board to perform                      •    安排會議以便董事會履行職責，
             its duties and responsibilities and to ensure all key and                      並確保董事會及時討論所有重要
             appropriate issues are discussed by the Board in a timely                      及適當事宜，而不會干擾本公司
             manner without interfering the Company’s operations;                           之營運；

     •       preparing meeting agendas after consultations with the                    •    經與董事總經理磋商後編製會議
             Managing Director;                                                             議程；

     •       exercising control over quality, quantity and timeliness of the           •    監控管理層與董事會之間的溝通
             flow of information between management and the Board and                       的質量、數量和及時性，確保董
             to ensure decisions are made on a fully informed basis by the                  事於完全知情情況下作出決定；
             Directors; and                                                                 及

     comprises a comprehensive organisation structure with assignment                面的組織架構，當中每個崗位都委以明
     of definite accountabilities and delegation of corresponding authorities        確責任，並授予相應權力。本集團根據
     to each post. Based on our organisation structure, a reporting system           組織架構建立了匯報制度，當中包括每
     has been developed including reporting channels from division heads             個主要業務單位的主管向董事會匯報的
     of business units to the Board.                                                 渠道。

     The risk management and internal control systems and accounting                 本集團設立風險管理及內部監控制度，
     system of the Group are aimed at identifying and evaluating                     以及會計制度，用以確認及評估本集團
     the Group’s risk and formulate risk mitigation strategies, and to               之風險，並制定減低風險之策略，以及
     provide reasonable assurance that assets are safeguarded against                合理確保資產不會在未經授權情況下被

     accordance with management’s authorisation, and the accounting                  權之情況下進行，以及會計賬目能夠可
     records are reliable for preparing financial information used within the        靠地被用作編製業務中可供刊發之財務

     of the new standard is not expected to have significant impact on                                及計量而言，預期應用該新準則不會對
     the financial performance and positions of the Group in terms of                                 本集團的財務表現及狀況造成重大影
     recognition and measurement. However, it is expected to affect the                               響。然而，預期其將影響綜合損益表的
     structure and presentation of the consolidated statement of profit or                            架構及呈列。
     loss.

            operating leases.                                                                經營租賃。

            or loss on a straight-line basis over the term of the relevant                   約協議年期以直線法於損益內確
            lease agreement. Initial direct costs incurred in negotiating                    認。磋商及安排經營租賃過程中

            of the reporting period.                                                         結束當日已頒佈或實際已頒佈之
                                                                                             稅率計算。

            bases used in the computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax                    確認。遞延稅項負債通常會就所

            probable that taxable profits will be available against which                    在可能出現可利用臨時差額扣稅

            deferred tax assets and liabilities are not recognised if the                    時差額確認。因業務合併以外交
            temporary difference arises from the initial recognition (other                  易初步確認資產及負債且不影響
            than in a business combination) of assets and liabilities in                     應課稅溢利亦不影響會計溢利而

            The effective interest method is a method of calculating                       實際利率法是一種計算金融資產
            the amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability                 或金融負債攤銷成本與相關期間
            and of allocating interest income and interest expense over                    分派利息收入及利息支出之方
            the relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate                   法。實際利率是於金融資產或金
            that exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and                      融負債的估計可使用年期或（如
            payments (including all fees and points paid or received that                  適用）更短期間將估計未來現金
            form an integral part of the effective interest rate, transaction              收入及付款（包括所有已付或已
            costs and other premiums or discounts) through the expected                    收並構成實際利率一部分之費
            life of the financial asset or financial liability, or, where                  用、交易成本及其他溢價或折
            appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying amount on                   價）精確折現至初步確認時之賬
            initial recognition.                                                           面淨值之比率。

            Interest income from debt instruments at FVTOCI and                            於本集團日常業務過程中來自按
            dividend income from equity instruments at FVTPL which are                     公平值計入其他全面收益之債務

            Financial assets                                                               金融資產
            All regular way purchases or sales of financial assets are                     所有日常買賣之金融資產於交易

             profit or loss.                                                              損益確認。

6.    SEGMENT INFORMATION                                                         6.   分部資料
      HKFRS 8 “Operating Segments” (“HKFRS 8”) requires operating                       香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 8 號「 經 營 分 部 」
      segments to be identified on the basis of internal reports about                 （「香港財務報告準則第8號」）要求主要
      components of the Group that are regularly reviewed by Chief                      營運決策者（「主要營運決策者」）在對分
      Operating Decision Maker (“CODM”) in order to allocate resources to               部作資源分配及評估其表現上所定期審
      segments and to assess their performance. Specifically, the Group’s               閱的有關本集團不同部門之內部報告作
      reportable segments under HKFRS 8 are organised into three main                   為確定經營分部之基準。具體而言，根
      operating divisions – (i) manufacturing and sale of laminates; (ii)               據香港財務報告準則第8號，本集團之
      properties; and (iii) investments.                                                須予呈報分部分為三個主要經營分部(i)

                                                                                        資。

      The accounting policies the Group used for segment reporting under               本集團根據香港財務報告準則第8號用
      HKFRS 8 are the same as those used in its HKFRS consolidated                     作分部呈報之會計政策，與其用於香港
      financial statements. Segment results represents the profit earned               財務報告準則之綜合財務報表一致。分
      or loss incurred by each segment with certain items not included                 部業績指除若干項目（未分配之公司收
      (unallocated corporate income and expenses and finance costs). This              入及支出以及融資成本）外，各分部所

      allocation and performance assessment.                                           主要營運決策者用於資源分配和績效評

      2026).                                                                            年：二零二六年）。

      proceeds of properties over prescribed direct costs. Prescribed                   地增值稅。指定直接成本界定為土地成

9.    INCOME TAX EXPENSE (continued)                                               9.   所得稅開支（續）
      Under the two-tiered profits tax rates regime of Hong Kong Profits                根據利得稅兩級制，合資格集團實體的
      Tax, the first HK$2 million of profits of the qualifying group entity will        首二百萬港元利潤將按8.25%徵稅，而
      be taxed at 8.25%, and profits above HK$2 million will be taxed at                超過二百萬港元的利潤則須按16.5%徵
      16.5%. The profits of group entities not qualifying for the two-tiered            稅。不符合利得稅兩級制的集團實體的
      profits tax rates regime will continue to be taxed at a flat rate of              利潤將繼續按16.5%的固定稅率徵稅。
      16.5%. Accordingly, the Hong Kong Profits Tax of the qualifying group             因此，合資格集團實體首二百萬港元估
      entity is calculated at 8.25% on the first HK$2 million of the estimated          計應課稅溢利將按8.25%的稅率繳納香
      assessable profits and at 16.5% on the estimated assessable profits               港利得稅，而二百萬港元以上之估計應
      above HK$2 million.                                                               課稅溢利則按16.5%的稅率繳稅。

      The Group has applied the temporary mandatory exception from                      本集團已針對補足稅的影響應用有關確
      recognising and disclosing deferred tax assets and liabilities for the            認及披露的遞延稅項資產及負債的臨時
      impacts of the top-up tax and accounts for it as a current tax when it is         強制豁免，並在產生時將其入賬列作即
      incurred.                                                                         期稅項。

      Taxation arising in other jurisdictions is calculated at the rates                其他司法權區之稅項乃按有關司法權區

                                                                                  年）。

      The Group is not exposed to foreign currency risk as a result of the        本集團不會因租賃安排而承擔外匯風
      lease arrangement, as all leases are denominated in the respective          險，因為所有租賃均以集團實體各自的
      functional currencies of group entities. The lease contracts do not         功能貨幣列值。租約並不包括餘值擔保
      contain residual value guarantee and/or lessee’s option to purchase         及╱或承租人於租期末購買物業的選擇
      the property at the end of lease term.                                      權。

      In determining the fair value of the relevant properties, the               為釐定相關物業的公平值，管理層就公
      management determines the appropriate valuation techniques and              平值計量釐定適當的估值技術及輸入數
      inputs for fair value measurements.                                         據。

      The Group engages an independent qualified valuer to perform its            本集團委聘獨立合資格估值師估值。管
      valuations. The management works closely with the independent               理層與獨立合資格估值師緊密合作，制
      qualified valuer to establish the appropriate valuation techniques and      定模型之適當估值技術及輸入數據。財
      inputs to the model. The persons in charge of financial matters report      務事宜負責人每年向董事匯報管理層之
      the management findings to the Directors annually to explain the            發現，闡明資產及負債公平值波動之因
      cause of fluctuations in the fair value of the assets and liabilities.      由。

31. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                        31. 資本風險管理
      The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group will           資本風險管理本集團管理其資本以確保
      be able to continue as a going concern while maximising the return to             通過優化債務與股本結存為權益持有人
      stakeholders through the optimisation of the debt and equity balance.             爭取最高回報，使本集團旗下實體能夠
      The Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged from prior year.                   持續經營。本集團之整體策略與過往年
                                                                                        度相同。

      The capital structure of the Group consists of bank borrowings                   誠如綜合權益變動表所披露，本集團
      and lease liabilities, net of cash and cash equivalents and equity               之資本結構包括銀行借貸及租賃負債
      attributable to owners of the Company, comprising issued share                   （扣除現金及現金等價物）及本公司持有
      capital, share premium, reserves and retained profits as disclosed in            人應佔權益（包括已發行股本、股份溢
      the consolidated statement of changes in equity.                                 價、儲備及保留溢利）。

      The Directors review the capital structure on a semi-annual basis.                董事每半年審閱資本結構。作為審閱一
      As part of this review, the Directors consider the cost of capital and            部分，董事將考慮資本成本，及與各類
      the risks associated with each class of capital. In the opinion of the            別資本相關之風險。董事認為，本集團
      Directors, the Group will balance its overall capital structure through           將通過支付股息、發行新股、購回股
      the payment of dividends, new share issues and share buy-backs as                 份、發行新債或者贖回現有債項，以平
      well as the issue of new debt or the redemption of existing debt.                 衡整體資本結構。

            Credit risk and impairment assessment                                         信貸風險及減值評估
            Credit risk refers to the risk that the Group’s counterparties                信貸風險指本集團交易對手因違
            default on their contractual obligations resulting in financial               反其合約義務而導致本集團產生
            losses to the Group. The Group’s maximum exposure                             財務虧損的風險。倘交易方於報
            to credit risk in the event of the counterparties’ failure to                 告期末未能履行彼等就各類已確
            perform their obligations at the end of the reporting period                  認金融資產之承擔，則本集團須
            in relation to each class of recognised financial assets is the               承受之最高信貸風險為於綜合財
            carrying amount of those assets as stated in the consolidated                 務狀況表所載該等資產賬面值。
            statement of financial position.

            Trade receivables arising from contracts with customers                       由客戶合約產生的貿易應收賬款
            In order to minimise the credit risk, the Directors has delegated             為將信貸風險降至最低，董事已
            the management to be responsible for determination of credit                  委派管理層負責制訂信貸限額、
            limits, credit approvals for determination of credit limits, credit           就制訂信貸限額給予的信貸審
            approvals and other monitoring procedures to ensure that                      批、信貸審批及其他監控程序，
            follow-up action is taken to recover overdue debts. In this                   以確保採取跟進措施收回逾期未
            regard, the Directors consider that the Group’s credit risk is                付之債項。就此而言，董事認
            significantly reduced.                                                        為，本集團之信貸風險已大幅降
                                                                                          低。

            The Group has no significant concentration of credit risk                     本集團並無高度集中之貿易應收
            on trade receivables with exposure spread over a number                       賬款信貸風險，風險由多個交易
            of counterparties and customers. However, the Group is                        方及客戶分攤。然而，本集團的
            exposed to the concentration on geographic segment in the                     風險集中於中國之地區分部。於
            PRC. At 31 December 2025, approximately 91% (2024: 92%)                       二零二五年十二月三十一日，本

                                                                                          之貿易應收賬款源自中國。

            Except for credit-impaired trade receivables which are                        除個別進行減值評估的信貸減值

            advice where appropriate.

      The Group participates in both a defined contribution scheme                     本集團同時參加一項根據職業退休計劃

      assets of the schemes are held separately from those of the Group                集團之資產分開持有，並投資於由獨立
